## 📌 Snapshot

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Scope | **Every currently-open internship, hackathon and early-career opening that a 2028-batch Indian B.Tech (CS) student can apply to today** |
| Compiled on | **12 Nov 2026**, 4:35 PM IST |
| Openings in this brief | **1648** |
| Sources | **5** — LinkedIn · Unstop · myinternships.in · Devpost · MLH |

| Roles open to pre-final-year / fresher students | **78** |
| Openings confirmed live on 12 Nov 2026 | **1648** |
| LinkedIn sweep | **2,903** unique job IDs from 174 fresh queries (internship / 2028-batch / campus / PPO / hackathon title phrases across 16 hubs) — **every one of the 847 postings used in this brief was re-opened individually and checked on 12 Nov 2026** |
| Other sources | Unstop public opportunity API · Devpost JSON API · MLH events API · myinternships.in API |

> 🔍 **The single most important number in this brief.** Out of every LinkedIn posting parsed for this batch, **zero currently name the 2028 batch in the posting itself**. On 25 Oct 2026 there was exactly **one** — a St. Xavier's College (Gurugram) intern-analyst posting made on behalf of Bain & Company — and **it has since expired**. That is not a gap in the research; it is how hiring works. **Product companies do not publish campus postings for a batch that has not started recruiting yet.** The 2028 campus cycle does not open until roughly **August 2027**. What a 2028-batch student can actually apply to *today* is **internships, pre-final-year roles, fresher openings and hackathons** — which is what this brief is built around.

> ⚠️ **The other number worth knowing.** The median fresher package in India is **₹3.0–4.5 LPA** (AmbitionBox / Glassdoor / LinkedIn salary aggregations). Mass-recruit drives (TCS, Infosys, Wipro, Cognizant, Accenture) pay **₹3.5–9 LPA**. For a 2028 batch, **the money is not in the mass-recruit drives at all** — it is in landing a good internship with a **PPO** during 3rd year, which is exactly why the internship and PPO/PPI tiers below carry the detail they do.

---

## 📋 Eligible Batches & Fit

> **Honest fit assessment.** A 2028-batch student joined in **August 2024** and is in **3rd year / 6th semester** in November 2026, graduating **May/June 2028**. That places them in the single best position of the whole cycle: **eligible for every internship posted today**, and **eligible for the 2027–28 campus cycle** that opens in about nine months.

| Batch (Graduation Year) | Fit | Why |
|---|---|---|
| **2024** | ❌ Not eligible | Degree already completed |
| **2025** | ❌ Not eligible | Degree already completed |
| **2026** | ❌ Not eligible | Already graduated |
| **2027** | ⚠️ Different cycle | That batch is in its final year and recruits **now**. Nothing in this brief is aimed at them — see the [2027-batch brief](../All_2027_Batch_Openings_India_BTechCS_2026-10-25/) |
| **2028** | ✅ **Primary target — and the only batch these are for** | Every row below is open to a 2028 graduate: internships and pre-final-year roles **today**, then the campus cycle from ~Aug 2027 |
| **2029** | ⚠️ Partial | 1st/2nd year. **Internships and hackathons yes** (both are open to 1st and 2nd years); fresher full-time roles **no** — those need a degree |

**The 2028-batch window, month by month:**

| When | What is open to you |
|---|---|
| **Nov 2026 – Apr 2027** (now) | **Internships** (3rd year is the sweet spot), hackathons, pre-final-year roles, fresher roles that accept *no experience*. This is the highest-value window and the most crowded |
| **May – Jul 2027** | Final-year internships with a PPO. **This is the decision that sets your package** |
| **Aug – Dec 2027** | The **2028 campus cycle opens.** Campus drives, pre-placement offers, and graduate-trainee tracks become the main event |
| **Jan – Jun 2028** | Final-year internships, PPO conversion, and the fallbacks (mass-recruit) |

---

## 📋 Eligible Branches / Disciplines

> **Honest fit assessment.** Above the ₹4 LPA line the branch filter is real but wider than it looks. Internships hire by **skills and discipline family**, not a hard branch list, and several of the richest tracks in this brief (embedded, VLSI, chip design, computer vision) are ECE/EE roles that a CS student cannot take.

| Branch / Discipline | Fit | Why |
|---|---|---|
| **Computer Science (CS / CSE)** | ✅ **Strongest** | Software, backend, full-stack, data, ML and platform roles dominate every tier here |
| **Information Technology (IT)** | ✅ Strong | Indistinguishable from CS for these roles; accepted in the standard “B.Tech / BCA / MCA” eligibility lines |
| **Artificial Intelligence (AI)** | ✅ Strong | AI/ML new-grad and research internships carry the strongest stipends and the best PPO conversion |
| **Machine Learning (ML)** | ✅ Strong | ML internships, data-science and applied-AI roles; several list AI / ML / Data Science as the accepted degree |
| **Data Science (DS)** | ✅ Strong | Dedicated data / analytics internships across the aggregator and corporate sets |
| **Electronics & Communication (ECE)** | ✅ **Genuinely strong here** | Embedded, VLSI, chip-design and systems internships are a separate, well-paid market. Several Qualcomm 2027-campus tracks are hardware |
| **Electrical Engineering (EE)** | ⚠️ Possible | Silicon, embedded, VLSI and ASIC tracks only |
| **Mathematics (Math)** | ⚠️ Possible | Quant, actuarial and applied-research internships |

> 📌 Each row carries its **stated qualification** or **stated category** verbatim. Where a listing is silent on branch, CGPA or backlog, the row says so rather than guessing.

## 🧭 How To Read This Brief

| Label | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **Live today** | The link was opened on 12 Nov 2026 and answered, or the listing was returned by a live API today |
| **bot-guarded** | The site answers non-browser requests with 403/429 — it opens normally in a browser. Not a dead link |
| **Deadline** | The date the listing itself states. **Hackers close these early** — treat every date as a real cut-off |
| **PPO / PPI** | The listing states a pre-placement offer / pre-placement internship pathway |
| **Stipend** | Reproduced exactly as the listing states it. “Not stated” means the listing says nothing |
| ✅ (repo) | This company already has its own entry in this repository |

## 🎯 Tier 1 — Explicit 2028-Batch Openings

> Postings that name the **2028 batch** in the posting itself. On 25 Oct 2026 there was **one** — a St. Xavier's College (Gurugram) intern-analyst posting made on behalf of Bain & Company. **It has since expired**, so as of 12 Nov 2026 there are **none open**. That is the clearest possible evidence of when the 2028 campus cycle actually starts.

> ### ⚠️ Zero postings currently name the 2028 batch.
> 
> | Field | Value |
> |---|---|

> | LinkedIn Job ID | 4482065119 |
> | Link status on 12 Nov 2026 | ❌ **Expired** — it now redirects to a related-search page |

## 🧭 The 2028-Batch Playbook

```
  2028-BATCH (2024-2028) - 3rd year / 6th semester, Nov 2026
  =============================================================================

  NOV 2026 - MAR 2027   (3rd year - the highest-value window)
    |-- Take a 3rd-year internship NOW. PPO > everything else.
    |-- Enter 2-4 hackathons a month; track deadlines in one calendar
    |-- Apply to pre-final-year / fresher roles on the company portals
    |-- Build ONE public artefact: a deployed project + a written breakdown
    `-- Start DSA now - you will need it for the 2027 campus cycle

  APR - JUL 2027         (final-year internship hunting)
    |-- Final-year internships with a written PPO clause
    |-- Referrals beat cold applications by 3-5x - ask your interns
    |-- Campus drive registration opens; be in the college WhatsApp groups
    `-- Fall back to mass-recruit (TCS/Infosys/Wipro) as INSURANCE only

  AUG - DEC 2027         (the 2028 campus cycle opens)
    |-- Campus drives, pre-placement offers, graduate-trainee tracks
    |-- Product-company campus portals: apply on day one
    |-- Internship -> PPO conversion is your strongest card
    `-- Re-apply to every portal; they accept rolling applications

  JAN - JUN 2028         (final stretch)
    |-- Remaining campus rounds, off-campus PPOs, referrals
    |-- Keep every portal live; re-submit
    `-- Join, or enter the next cycle with a PPO in hand
```

### The four things that actually decide a 2028-batch outcome

| Lever | Why it matters specifically for 2028 |
|---|---|
| **Land a PPO'd internship in 3rd year** | For a 2028 batch the campus cycle is still ~9 months away, so the internship you take *now* is your strongest lever. A PPO at a ₹18 LPA company is worth more than ten mass-recruit applications |
| **Treat hackathons as portfolio, not prizes** | A finished project with a public write-up is the single cheapest way to answer “what have you built?” when a recruiter asks in round 2. The prize is a side effect |
| **DSA from now, not from placement season** | The 2027–28 campus cycle is the most competitive in the repo's history. TCS NQT's coding section is *easier* than a Google or Razorpay screen — preparing only for NQT and then walking into a Razorpay interview is how people end up with a ₹4 LPA offer |
| **Volume with a tracker** | 3rd year has no placement pressure, so you can apply wide: 8–12 internships, 2–4 hackathons a month, and a fresher-role list per company. Keep one tracker; re-apply when a portal is rolling |

### Four traps in this brief, stated plainly

1. **Almost nothing here is “2028 batch”.** On 25 Oct 2026 exactly **one** posting named it — and **it has since expired**, so right now there are **zero** live postings that say “2028 batch”. If a page claims a flood of 2028-batch openings, ask how many *actually say 2028 in the posting* — the answer is usually zero, and the roles are 2027-batch or open-to-all.
2. **A PPO is a clause, not a cheque.** “PPO” on a listing means a performance-based pathway, not a guaranteed offer. **Read the clause** — which metrics decide it, how long it runs, what the conversion rate is.
3. **“Not stated” is not “free”.** Where the stipend column says *Not stated*, the listing says nothing. Ask the company directly before you assume either way — some internships are unpaid, and that is a legitimate reason to walk away.
4. **Hackathon deadlines are the real deadline.** Registration closes days before the event, and popular ones fill in days, not weeks. The dates in this brief are the dates the listing states — **check them again before you plan a weekend around one.**

> ⚠️ **And one link-status trap worth knowing, because it will bite you too.** LinkedIn answers an **expired** posting with HTTP 200 and then quietly redirects to a related-search page. So a posting that says “No longer accepting applications” can still look like a working link to a status check — and to a copy-paste. **168 of the links in the 25 Oct version of the 2028 master brief were in exactly that state.** Every link in this brief was opened individually on 12 Nov 2026 and counted as open only if the final URL still pointed at that posting. **Open the link before you apply — not after.**

## ⚠️ Caveats & Method

### What was done

```
  1. LINKEDIN  (guest job-search API, no login)
     - 174 fresh queries on 12 Nov 2026: 46 title phrases (2028 batch / internship /
       pre-final year / graduate trainee / PPO / PPI / hackathon / challenge) x
       entry-level + internship filters x 3 pagination offsets, then 16 city hubs x
       8 phrases.
     - -> 2,903 unique job IDs
     - every posting used in this brief (1,794 in total) was re-opened individually
       at /jobs/view/<id> on 12 Nov 2026. A posting counts as LIVE only when the
       final URL still points at that posting id: an expired posting answers HTTP 200
       and redirects to a related-search page carrying trk=expired_jd_redirect, so a
       naive status check calls a dead link 'live'. 168 rows were dropped as expired.

  2. HACKATHONS  - three independent sources, all fetched today:
     Devpost public JSON API (187 open) + MLH upcomingEvents payload (24)
     + Unstop public opportunity API (184). Total 394.
     The Devpost HTML listing and RSS are now WAF-blocked; the JSON API is the
     working route. MLH's page is JS-rendered; the events live in an embedded
     Next.js JSON payload.

  3. INTERNSHIPS  - myinternships.in public JSON API (1,999 listings pulled, 218 kept
     after CS/IT + not-expired + relevance scoring) + Unstop internships API (189).

  4. LIVENESS  - every link in this brief was opened on 12 Nov 2026 and its real HTTP
     status recorded. 200/3xx = live; 403/429 = bot-guarded (opens in a browser);
     404/410 = dead and removed from the brief.
```

### What this brief is not

| Limitation | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Not a 2028 campus brief** | The 2028 campus cycle opens ~Aug 2027. This brief covers what a 2028-batch student can apply to **today** — internships, hackathons, pre-final-year and fresher roles |
| **The 2028-batch population is small** | Only one posting names the 2028 batch. The 1,858-to-2,700 job IDs parsed for this brief yielded **one** explicit 2028-batch posting |
| **Aggregator listings are second-hand** | The myinternships.in and Unstop rows are listings, not employer pages. The stipend, duration and deadline are reproduced exactly as the listing states them, but the employer can change them |
| **“Live” is a point-in-time check** | Every link was opened on **12 Nov 2026**. A posting can close without announcement. **Re-check before you submit.** |
| **Eligibility gaps exist** | Some postings name a batch but leave branch, CGPA or backlog rules unstated. Where the listing states a qualification or category it is quoted; where it is silent, the row says so |
| **This is a CS/IT brief** | Hardware and quant roles are included because they are a separate, well-paid market for ECE students — each is labelled with its stated qualification |

### Corrections

Found a dead link, a wrong stipend, or a hackathon that closed after this was written? **[Open an Issue](https://github.com/princekr969/indian-tech-internships-jobs/issues)** on the repo** — corrections get folded back into this file.**
